# 要补中文释义的常用词（按词频排，前 8000 个）：词头	词性（性）	英文释义（前两个义项）
de	adp	of, 's, used after the thing owned and before the owner | from (with the source or provenance of or at)
el	det	masculine singular definite article, the | feminine singular definite article used before nouns which start with a stressed /a/
que	pron	who, that | that, whom, which
en	adp	in, at, on | in (a time)
a	adp	to | by
la	pron	accusative of ella, ello (when the antecedent's implied gender is feminine), and usted (when referring to a woman), her, it, you (formal) | impersonal neuter pronoun (accusative) in certain colloquial phrases: it, this
un	det	an, a
se	pron	A reflexive or reciprocal pronoun: oneself, himself, herself, itself, yourself, themselves, yourselves, each other, one another | A pronoun used with transitive verbs to create the passive voice
uno	det	one
con	adp	with | Dependent preposition following certain verbs.
del	adp	of the, from the (+ a masculine noun in singular).
tener	verb	to have, to possess | to have, to possess, to be (a condition or quality)
suyo	det	his, hers, its, to her | theirs, their, to them
para	adp	for, to (expressing a recipient) | to, in order to, so, for (expressing the intended purpose of an action)
su	det	(before the noun) apocopic form of suyo | (before the noun) used to express an approximate number: about, approximately
por	adp	by | for (indicates something given in an exchange)
ir	verb	to go (away from speaker and listener) | (said of things or people) to be suitable or apt for its purpose, to match or suit well (of clothing)
este	det	this
lo	det	neuter definite article used only before nominalized adjectives: the, that which is
todo	det	all, every | each, every
mío	det	(after the noun) mine, my, of mine
como	sconj	as (introducing a basis of comparison or equality) | as, since (being that)
si	sconj	if, whether | (after pero) points out a contradiction (and often implies that it's obvious or that it's easy to find out about)
al	adp	contraction of a + el, literally “at the, to the” | (followed by a verb) upon
las	det	feminine plural definite article, the
las	pron	accusative of ellas, them | accusative of ustedes (when referring to more than one woman), you all (formal)
qué	pron	what
tuyo	det	yours, your
ese	det	that
ése	pron	that one | the former
o	cconj	or | either … or
bien	adv	well (adverbial form of bueno) | well, fine, okay (state of being satisfied with the status quo)
ya	adv	(in the present or imperative) now, right now, (in the negative) anymore, no longer | (in the present or imperative) by now, at this point
otro	det	other, another
éste	pron	this one | (with an article) substitutes a noun one can't remember or can't remember the name of
dar	verb	to give, to give out | to hand over
cuando	sconj	when
aquí	adv	here (in the speaker's location)
le	pron	to him, for him, dative of él | to her, for her, dative of ella
creer	verb	to believe | to think, to believe, to reckon, to be of the opinion
mucho	det	much, a lot of | many, a lot of
solo	adv	only, solely, just
porque	sconj	because
pasar	verb	to pass, to hand, to slip | to happen
sobre	adp	over, above, on, upon, atop | about
algo	pron	something, anything
bueno	intj	okay, fine | (used at the start of a phrase) well, a short pause in a sentence
así	adv	like this, like that, as such, thus, so, thereby, this way, that way | (followed by a subjunctive clause) used to express wishes of misfortune against someone, or to cast a spell
pensar	verb	to think, ponder | to reflect, think through
cómo	adv	how?
de la	det	of the
hace	adp	ago (Note: unlike in English, hace precedes the number.) | (followed by que) in, it has been...since (a past period of time)
desde	adp	since (from a specified time in the past) | from (a location)
venir	verb	to come (move closer to some location (most often closer to the speaker), by default the speaker's location) | to arrive
llevar	verb	to take somewhere | to take away or take with oneself
vida	noun f	life | lifestyle
encontrar	verb	to meet | to find, encounter, come across
tomar	verb	to take (put under one's physical or mental control, often in one's hands) | to draw from, take, derive
quedar	verb	to be (as a result of something), to end up | to be situated, to be located (used with only static objects, such as buildings)
mejor	adv	better
mirar	verb	to look at (to try to see, to pay attention to with one’s eyes) | to watch (to look at, view, or observe for a period of time)
salir	verb	to go out, to leave, to depart, to head out | to go out, to come out (to leave one's abode to go to public places)
ni	cconj	Used when negating two or more elements, and not, not A or B, not A nor B, neither A nor B
lo que	pron	what, that which (the thing that) | whatever
lo que	sconj	but
hasta	adp	until | up to, to the point of, as much as
entonces	adv	then, next, thereupon, at that time, at that point | then, therefore, so, thus, in that case
a la	adp	a la, in the style or manner of | to the
antes	adv	earlier, before, sooner, previously, formerly, beforehand (at an earlier time) | soon (in certain phrases)
poco	adv	little (not much)
chico	noun m	boy, kid | guy, lad
quién	pron	who?, whom?, (with “de”) whose? | (followed by a subjunctive clause in the imperfect or pluperfect tense) if only, I wish
problema	noun m	problem, issue, challenge (a difficulty or obstacle that has to be dealt with) | trouble (in the singular or in the plural)
quien	pron	who | (construed with a) whom
lugar	noun m	place
elle	pron	they, them (singular), a gender-neutral singular third-person personal pronoun
mí	pron	me, (declined form of yo used as the object of a preposition)
país	noun m	(with or without a) country (the territory of a nation) | (with or without a) country, land (a set region of land having particular human occupation or agreed limits)
buscar	verb	to seek, search for, look for, try to find, to scour | to look up (in a search engine, dictionary, etc.)
tipo	noun m	kind, type, sort, manner | rate, interest rate
último	adj	final, last | latest, most recent
nadie	pron	no one, nobody (anyone or anybody)
oír	verb	to hear | to hear (passively perceive a sound)
he	adv	here is | behold (+ aquí)
forma	noun f	shape, form, figure | way, manner
tratar	verb	to treat (to handle, behave toward in a specific way) | to treat (to apply medical care)
vivir	verb	to live, to be alive | to make a living, to live on
cuenta	noun f	count, tally | operation
momento	noun m	moment, instant | timing
entender	verb	to understand, to fathom | to hear
durante	adp	during, in, over | for (as a duration)
vez	noun f	time, instance | place (in a queue)
ahí	adv	there (away from the speaker) | that (in certain phrases)
trabajar	verb	to work | to work in, work on, to workshop (+ en)
oh	intj	oh (expression of awe, surprise, pain or realization)
ayudar	verb	to help
mismo	adj	same, identical, one and the same, indicates that the two compared noun clauses both represent the one thing | similar, alike, practically identical, indicates that the two compared noun clauses have one or more matching qualities
mal	adv	badly, poorly, ill | awry, amiss, wrong, wrongly
mayor	adj	comparative degree of grande: bigger | comparative degree of viejo: older, elder
matar	verb	to kill, slay | to put out, extinguish (a flame or light)
entrar	verb	to enter, to come in, to get in, to go in, to get inside, to come on in | to break in
mismo	adv	right, exactly, immediately
usar	verb	to use | to wear
ninguno	det	no, none, (no/not…) at all, whatsoever, in the slightest, any (negative)
pues	sconj	so, then, in that case | because, since
propio	adj	own (belonging to) | proper
contra	adp	against, versus
cual	pron	suchlike, like (which), such as
fuera	adv	outside | out
buena	noun f	inheritance
razón	noun f	reason | reasoning
cambiar	verb	to change, to shift, to switch, to alter | to modify
importante	adj	important, that cares, that matters.
nacional	adj	national, nationwide (of or relating to a nation) | national (native to a nation)
partido	noun m	party | game, match (chiefly for soccer)
grupo	noun m	group | party, team, crew, gang, squad, (when interchangeable with "group")
pasado	noun m	past, bygones | past
allí	adv	there (away from the speaker and the listener)
ganar	verb	to gain | to earn
casi	adv	almost
hacia	adp	toward, towards | around, about (indicating time)
dicho	det	said
recordar	verb	to remember, to recollect | to remember
cualquiera	det	any, whatever, whichever

recibir	verb	to receive, get | to welcome, to greet, to receive (guests, patients)
equipo	noun m	team | equipment, kit, hardware
uno	pron	one
sistema	noun m	system | scheme (a systematic plan of future action; also, a group of independent but interrelated elements comprising a unified whole)
traer	verb	to bring (in the sense of transport, for differences with llevar and examples see that entry) | to bring about, cause, occasion
social	adj	social
contar	verb	to count, to tally | to tell (a story), narrate
público	adj	public
muerto	adj	(with estar) dead | (with estar) exhausted, dead tired
millón	noun m	million (10⁶)
permitir	verb	to allow, permit | to afford, to allow
pueblo	noun m	town, village | the common people, the working classes
política	noun f	politics (activities associated with running a government or organisation) | policy (a formal principle or protocol of conduct)
suponer	verb	to suppose, to surmise, to assume, to guess, to imagine, to figure, to say, to take it | to presume, to expect
de que	sconj	that (introduces a clause)
realmente	adv	really, actually, genuinely
aquel	det	that (over there; implying some distance)
importar	verb	to matter, to be important [with a ‘someone’] (often translated idiomatically with English care or mind, with subject and object reversed) | (in the negative) to care
varios	det	various, several
olvidar	verb	to forget | to forget
después	adv	later, afterwards, afterward, post | next
